1995 No. 2705 (N.I. 15)

The Jobseekers (Northern Ireland) Order 1995 [^c16983371][^c-50a50d46ef4c1e5186d7da37e24287c3][^c-9fcf6e087691f55f48fe64bd03b01097][^c-773218e9e5d9220273b865b08a9ab25a][^c-524bb7fd5fcfdea13a6710082542e4e7][^c-4cc8a1031407feab6ec14f23618c85fe][^c-7e117a7006fed2d608f35a17e7be9f14][^c-35b4f853238dccf86608530909d2db1a]

[^c16983381]18th October 1995 [^c20625831][^c20625851][^c21408941][^c22545741]

## Part I — Introductory

### 1. Title and commencement

(1) This Order may be cited as the Jobseekers (Northern Ireland) Order 1995.

(2) This Order comes into operation on such day or days as the Department may by order appoint[^c16983391].

### 2. Interpretation

(1) The Interpretation Act (Northern Ireland) 1954[^c16983401] applies to Article 1 and the following provisions of this Order as it applies to a Measure of the Northern Ireland Assembly.

(2)[^c-14874fd0c153c54c4d511424c38b5240] In this Order—

- [^c21784681]\[“adjudication officer” means an adjudication officer appointed under section 36 of the Administration Act;\]
- [^c16983421]“the Administration Act” means the Social Security Administration (Northern Ireland) Act 1992;
- “applicable amount” means the applicable amount determined in accordance with regulations under Article 6;
- “benefit year” has the meaning given by Article 4(4);
- [^c16983431]“the Benefits Act” means the Social Security Contributions and Benefits (Northern Ireland) Act 1992;
- “child” means a person under the age of 16;
- “claimant” means a person who claims a jobseeker's allowance except that in relation to a joint-claim couple claiming a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance it means the couple, or each member of the couple, as the context requires; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540][^c16983441];
- “contribution-based conditions” means the conditions set out in Article 4;
- “contribution-based jobseeker's allowance” has the meaning given in Article 3(4); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540]
- “couple” means—[^c-3e5a57af203e6e7e47efcb5ff305f23e]
  - (a) two people who are married to, or civil partners of, each other and are members of the same household, or
  - (b) two people who are not married to, or civil partners of, each other but are living together as if spouses of each other otherwise than in prescribed circumstances;
- “the Department” means the Department for Communities[^c-cc613883fca5b1d60bd9c6cb219b056f] ;
- “employed earner” has the meaning prescribed for the purposes of this Order;
- “employment”, except in Article 9, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-91a6ca754a98226ad50be3bef22f9882] has the meaning prescribed for the purposes of this Order;
- “employment officer”, for any purpose of this Order, means an officer of the Department or the Department for Employment and Learning or such other person as may be designated for that purpose by an order made by the Department;[^c-f6d99a9c718264bb494fe85b5dad958d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-91a6ca754a98226ad50be3bef22f9882]
- “entitled”, in relation to a jobseeker's allowance, is to be construed in accordance with—
  - (a) the provisions of this Order relating to entitlement; and
  - (b) section 1 of the Administration Act and Article 27 of the Social Security (Northern Ireland) Order 1998[^c21253671] ;
- “family” means—
  - (a) a couple[^c16983491];
  - (b) a couple and a member of the same household for whom one of them is, or both are, responsible and who is a child or a person of a prescribed description;
  - (c) except in prescribed circumstances, a person who is not a member of a couple and a member of the same household for whom that person is responsible and who is a child or a person of a prescribed description;
- “[^c21253691]**FAS payments**” means payments made under the Financial Assistance Scheme Regulations 2005;
- “income-based conditions” means the conditions set out in Article 5;
- “income-based jobseeker's allowance” has the meaning given in Article 3(4); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540]
- “[^c21253721]**income-related employment and support allowance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540]**” means an income-related allowance under Part 1 of the Welfare Reform Act (Northern Ireland) 2007 (employment and support allowance); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540]
- “jobseeker's agreement” has the meaning given by Article 11(1);
- “jobseeker’s direction” has the meaning given by Article 21A;[^c-7dbe03ca8dd0e3e7ac8e6b1c52ad9285] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-91a6ca754a98226ad50be3bef22f9882]
- “jobseeking period” has the meaning prescribed for the purposes of this Order;
- “joint-claim couple” and “joint-claim jobseeker's allowance” have the meanings given by Article 3(4); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540]
- *Definition rep. by 2004 c. 33*
- “National Insurance Fund” means the Northern Ireland National Insurance Fund;
- “the nominated member”, in relation to a joint-claim couple, shall be construed in accordance with Article 5B(4); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-7b0ab0b146ef8d2ce3f8621ef23b2540]
- “Northern Ireland” includes the territorial waters of the United Kingdom adjacent to Northern Ireland;
- [^c16983591]“occupational pension scheme” has the same meaning as it has in the Pension Schemes (Northern Ireland) Act 1993 by virtue of section 1 of that Act;
- “pensionable age” has the meaning prescribed for the purposes of this Order;
- “pension payments” means—
  - (a) periodical payments made in relation to a person, under a personal pension scheme or, in connection with the coming to an end of an employment of his, under an occupational pension scheme or a public service pension scheme; and
  - (b) such other payments as may be prescribed;
- “personal pension scheme” means—
  - (a) a personal pension scheme as defined by section 1 of the Pension Schemes (Northern Ireland) Act 1993;
  - (b) an annuity contract or trust scheme approved under section 620 or 621 of the Income and Corporation Taxes Act 1988 or a substituted contract within the meaning of section 622(3) of that Act which is treated as having become a registered pension scheme by virtue of paragraph 1(1)(f) of Schedule 36 to the Finance Act 2004; and[^c21253711]
  - (c) a personal pension scheme approved under Chapter 4 of Part 14 of the Income and Corporation Taxes Act 1988 which is treated as having become a registered pension scheme by virtue of paragraph 1(1)(g) of Schedule 36 to the Finance Act 2004;
- “[^c21253701]**PPF payments**” means any payments made in relation to a person—
  - (a) payable under the pension compensation provisions as specified in Article 146(2) of the Pensions (Northern Ireland) Order 2005 or section 162(2) of the Pensions Act 2004 (the pension compensation provisions); or
  - (b) payable under Article 150 of the Pensions (Northern Ireland) Order 2005 or section 166 of the Pensions Act 2004 (duty to pay scheme benefits unpaid at assessment date etc.);
- “prescribed”, except in Article 29 (and in Article 36 so far as relating to regulations under Article 29)[^c16983611] means specified in or determined in accordance with regulations;
- “public service pension scheme” has the same meaning as it has in the Pension Schemes (Northern Ireland) Act 1993 by virtue of section 1 of that Act;
- “regulations”, except in Article 29 (and in Article 36 so far as relating to regulations under Article 29) means regulations made by the Departments;
- “tax year” means the 12 months beginning with 6th April in any year;
- “trade dispute” means any dispute between employers and employees, or between employees and employees, which is connected with the employment or non-employment or the terms of employment or the conditions of employment of any persons, whether employees in the employment of the employer with whom the dispute arises, or not;
- “training” has the meaning prescribed for the purposes of this Order and, in relation to prescribed provisions of this Order, if regulations so provide, includes assistance to find training or employment, or to improve a person's prospects of being employed, of such a kind as may be prescribed; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-91a6ca754a98226ad50be3bef22f9882]
- *Definition rep. by 2004 c. 33*
- “week” means a period of 7 days beginning with a Sunday or such other period of 7 days as may be prescribed;
- “work” has the meaning prescribed for the purpose of this Order;
- “[^c-09a92d0414bf3a7acd5e77ca9fd90f5d]**work availability requirement**” has the meaning given by Article 8E;
- “**work preparation requirement**” has the meaning given by Article 8C;
- “**work search requirement**” has the meaning given by Article 8D;
- “**work-focused interview requirement**” has the meaning given by Article 8B;
- “**work-related requirement**” has the meaning given by Article 8;
- “year”, except in the expression “benefit year”, means a tax year.

(2A)[^c-33e11e9314f7202f616b18fde036db51] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(3) The expressions “ limited capability for work[^c21253751]”, “linked period”, “relevant education” and “remunerative work” are to be read with paragraphs 2, 3, 14 and 1 of Schedule 1.

(4) Subject to any regulations made for the purposes of this paragraph, “earnings” is to be construed for the purpose of this Order in accordance with section 3 of the Benefits Act and paragraph 6 of Schedule 1 to this Order.

## Part II — The Jobseeker's Allowance

### Entitlement

#### 3. The jobseeker's allowance

(1) An allowance, to be known as a jobseeker's allowance, shall be payable in accordance with the provision of this Order.

(2) Subject to the provisions of this Order, a claimant is entitled to a jobseeker's allowance if he—

- (a) is available for employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-2c3d2a8d46cb12b8eaec3fa2fa230c4f]
- (b) has accepted a claimant commitment[^c-a2ceb9bfdc7b191231f514ffe97799a5] ;
- (c) is actively seeking employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-2c3d2a8d46cb12b8eaec3fa2fa230c4f]
- (d)[^c16983711] satisfies the conditions set out in Article 4;
- (e) is not engaged in remunerative work;
- (f)[^c20626291] does not have limited capability for work;
- (g) is not receiving relevant education;
- (h) is under pensionable age; and
- (i) is in Northern Ireland

(2A) Subject to the provisions of this Order, a claimant who is not a member of a joint-claim couple is entitled to a jobseeker's allowance if he satisfies— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

- (a) the conditions set out in sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) and (e) to (i) of paragraph (2); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]
- (b) the conditions set out in Article 5. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

(2B) Subject to the provisions of this Order, a joint-claim couple are entitled to a jobseeker's allowance if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

- (a) a claim for the allowance is made jointly by the couple; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]
- (b) each member of the couple satisfies the conditions set out in sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) and (e) to (i) of paragraph (2); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]
- (c) the conditions set out in Article 5A are satisfied in relation to the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

(2C) Regulations may prescribe circumstances in which paragraph (2A) is to apply to a claimant who is a member of a joint-claim couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

(2D) Regulations may, in respect of cases where a person would (but for the regulations) be a member of two or more joint-claim couples, make provision for only one of those couples to be a joint-claim couple; and the provision which may be so made includes provision for the couple which is to be the joint-claim couple to be nominated— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

- (a) by the persons who are the members of the couples, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]
- (b) in default of one of the couples being so nominated, by the Department. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-d5e108782a3205948d64d3b6fb19c4e9]

(3) A jobseeker's allowance is payable in respect of a week.

(4) In this Order— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]

- [^c16983711]“a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance” means a jobseeker's allowance entitlement to which is based on the claimant's satisfying conditions which include those set out in Article 4;. . . *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]
- “an income-based jobseeker's allowance” means a jobseeker's allowance entitlement to which is based on the claimant's satisfying conditions which include those set out in Article 5. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6] or a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]
- “a joint-claim couple” means a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6] couple[^c16983791] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6] who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]
  - (a) are not members of any family whose members include a person in respect of whom a member of the couple is entitled to child benefit, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]
  - (b) are of a prescribed description; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]
- “a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance” means a jobseeker's allowance entitlement to which arises by virtue of paragraph (2B). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a44c3d8f0f2acbd759dcf677ccea5de6]

#### 4. The contribution-based conditions[^c-6f9168ac42ea6400ad60bf6d04b95966][^c-e85605a7a1882e1105eef5b3ecc595bd]

(1) The conditions referred to in Article 3(2)(d)[^c16983821] are that the claimant—

- (a) has actually paid Class 1 contributions in respect of one ( “the base year”) of the last two complete years before the beginning of the relevant benefit year and satisfies the additional conditions set out in paragraph (2);
- (b) has, in respect of the last two complete years before the beginning of the relevant benefit year, either paid Class 1 contributions or been credited with earnings and satisfies the additional condition set out in paragraph (3);
- (c) does not have earnings in excess of the prescribed amount; and
- (d) is not entitled to income support.

(2) The additional conditions mentioned in paragraph (1)(a) are that—

- (a) the contributions have been paid before the week for which the jobseeker's allowance is claimed;
- (b)[^c22545841] the claimant's relevant earnings for the base year upon which primary Class 1 contributions have been paid or treated as paid are not less than the base year's lower earnings limit multiplied by 26.

(2A)[^c22545811] Regulations may make provision for the purposes of paragraph (2)(b) for determining the claimant's relevant earnings for the base year.

(2B) Regulations under paragraph (2A) may, in particular, make provision—

- (a) for making that determination by reference to the amount of a person's earnings for periods comprised in the base year;
- (b) for determining the amount of a person's earnings for any such period by—
  - (i) first determining the amount of the earnings for the period in accordance with regulations made for the purposes of section 3(2) of the Benefits Act, and
  - (ii) then disregarding so much of the amount found in accordance with head (i) as exceeded the base year's lower earnings limit (or the prescribed equivalent).

(3) The additional condition mentioned in paragraph (1)(b) is that the earnings factor derived from so much of the claimant's earnings as did not exceed the upper earnings limit and[^c16983861] upon which primary Class 1 contributions have been paid or treated as paid or from so much of the claimant's earnings as did not exceed the upper earnings limit and credited is not less, in each of the two complete years, than the lower earnings limit for the year multiplied by 50.

(3A)[^c16983901] Where primary Class 1 contributions have been paid or treated as paid on any part of a person's earnings, paragraph (3)[^c22545861] shall have effect as if such contributions had been paid or treated as paid on so much of the earnings as did not exceed the upper earnings limit.

(3B)[^c22545891] Regulations may—

- (a) provide for the first set of conditions to be taken to be satisfied in the case of persons—
  - (i) who have been entitled to any prescribed description of benefit during any prescribed period or at any prescribed time, or
  - (ii) who satisfy other prescribed conditions;
- (b) with a view to securing any relaxation of the requirements of the first set of conditions in relation to persons who have been entitled as mentioned in sub-paragraph (a)(i), provide for that set of conditions to apply in relation to them subject to prescribed modifications.

(3C) In paragraph (3B)—

- “**the first set of conditions**” means the condition set out in paragraph (1)(a) and the additional conditions set out in paragraph (2);
- “**benefit**” means—
  - (za) universal credit;[^c-808c2a51785c7ef7547dace3b8a022b7]
  - (a) any benefit within the meaning of section 121(1) of the Benefits Act,
  - (b) any benefit under Parts 7 to 12 of the Benefits Act,
  - (c) credits under regulations under section 22(5) of the Benefits Act,
  - (d) a contribution-based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c2cbafd6621bd850fc9e12cda9eb95b9] jobseeker's allowance, and
  - (e) working tax credit.

(4) For the purposes of this Article—

- (a) “benefit year” means a period which is a benefit year for the purposes of Part II of the Benefits Act or such other period as may be prescribed for the purposes of this Article;
- (b) “the relevant benefit year” is the benefit year which includes—
  - (i) the beginning of the jobseeking period which includes the week for which a jobseeker's allowance is claimed, or
  - (ii) (if earlier) the beginning of any linked period; and
- (c) other expressions which are used in this Article and the Benefits Act have the same meaning in this Article as they have in the Act.

#### 5. The income-based conditions

(1)[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] The conditions referred to in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] Article 3(2A)(b)[^c16983921] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] are that the claimant— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

- (a) has an income which does not exceed the applicable amount (determined in accordance with regulations under Article 6) or has no income; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (b) is not entitled to income support *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712], state pension credit or an income-related employment and support allowance[^c20626311] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712];
- (c) is not a member of a family one of whose members is entitled to income support; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (d) is not a member of a family one of whose members is entitled to an income-based jobseeker's allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (dd (affected by repeal, see note))[^c16983961][^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] is not a member of a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] couple[^c16983971] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] the other member of which is entitled to state pension credit; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (de (affected by repeal, see note))[^c20626331][^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] is not a member of a couple the other member of which is entitled to an income-related employment and support allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (e) is not a member of a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] couple *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] the other member of which is engaged in remunerative work; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (f) is a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
  - (i) who has reached the age of 18; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] in respect of whom a direction under Article 18 is in force; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] who has, in prescribed circumstances to be taken into account for a prescribed period, reached the age of 16 but not the age of 18. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(2) Regulations may provide for one or both of the following conditions to be included in the income-based conditions, in the case of a person to whom paragraph (1)(f)(ii) or (iii) applies— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

- (a) a condition that the claimant must register for employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (b) a condition that the claimant must register for training. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(3) In paragraph (1)(f)(iii) “period” includes— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

- (a) a period fo a determinate length; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (b) a period defined by reference to the happening of a future event; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (c) a period of a determinate length but subject to earlier determination upon the happening of a future event. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(4) Regulations under paragraph (2) may, in particular, make provision by reference to persons designated by the Department for the purpose of the regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

#### 5A. The conditions for claims by joint-claim couples[^c20626371]

(1) The conditions referred to in Article 3(2B)(c) are— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

- (a) that the income of the joint-claim couple does not exceed the applicable amount (determined in accordance with regulations under Article 6) or the couple have no income; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (b) that no member of a family of which the couple are members is entitled to income support; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (c) that no member of any such family (other than the couple) is entitled to an income-based jobseeker's allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (cc (affected by repeal, see note))[^c16984021][^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] that neither member of the couple is entitled to state pension credit; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (cd (affected by repeal, see note))[^c20626351][^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] that neither member of the couple is entitled to an income-related employment and support allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (d) that at least one member of the couple has reached the age of 18; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
- (e) that if only one member of the couple has reached the age of 18, the other member of the couple is a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
  - (i) in respect of whom a direction under Article 18 is in force; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712] who has, in prescribed circumstances to be taken into account for a prescribed period, reached the age of 16. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(2) Paragraphs (2) and (4) of Article 5 shall apply in relation to a member of the couple to whom paragraph (1)(e)(i) or (ii) applies as they apply in relation to a claimant to whom paragraph (1)(f)(ii) or (iii) of that Article applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(3) In paragraph (1)(e)(ii) “period” shall be construed in accordance with Article 5(3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

#### 5B. Joint-claim couples: the nominated member

(1) Where a joint-claim couple make a claim for a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, they may nominate one of them as the member of the couple to whom the allowance is to be payable. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(2) In default of one of them being so nominated, the allowance shall be payable to whichever of them is nominated by the Department. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(3) Paragraphs (1) and (2) have effect subject to Article 6A(4) and (7). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(4) In this Order references to the nominated member of a joint-claim couple are, except where Article 22A(7) applies, to the member of the couple nominated under paragraph (1) or (2); and where Article 22A(7) applies, references to the nominated member of such a couple are to the member of the couple to whom Article 22A(7) provides for the allowance to be payable. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

(5) Nothing in this Article or Article 22A(7) affects the operation of any statutory provision by virtue of which any amount of the allowance is required or authorised to be paid to someone other than the nominated member of the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c958e911b46172d1b8be08147e265712]

#### 6. Amount payable by way of a jobseeker's allowance

(1) In the case of a contribution-based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a202ca4a7bf706997ad90395624c1167] jobseeker's allowance, the amount payable in respect of a claimant ( “his personal rate”) shall be calculated by—

- (a) determining the age-related amount applicable to him; and
- (b) making prescribed deductions in respect of earnings , pension payments,[^c19914841] PPF payments and FAS payments .

(2) The age-related amount applicable to a claimant, for the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), shall be determined in accordance with regulations.

(3) In the case of an income-based jobseeker's allowance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1] (other than a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance)[^c16984041] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1], the amount payable shall be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1]

- (a) if a claimant has no income, the applicable amount; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1]
- (b) if a claimant has an income, the amount by which the applicable amount exceeds his income. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1]

(3A) In the case of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, the amount payable in respect of a joint-claim couple shall be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1]

- (a) if the couple have no income, the applicable amount; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1]
- (b) if the couple have an income, the amount by which the applicable amount exceeds the couple's income. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-0102ec14b568b0c6a2266453d4a7f4f1]

(4) Except in prescribed circumstances, a jobseeker's allowance shall not be payable where the amount otherwise payable would be less than a prescribed minimum.

(5) The applicable amount shall be such amount or the aggregate of such amounts as may be determined in accordance with regulations.

(6) Where a claimant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] is entitled to both a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance and an income-based jobseeker's allowance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] but has no income, the amount payable *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] by way of a jobseeker's allowance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] shall be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

- (a) the applicable amount, if that is greater than his personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]
- (b) his personal rate, if it is not. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(7) Where the amount payable to a claimant to whom paragraph (6) apples is the applicable amount, the amount payable to him by way of a jobseeker's allowance shall be taken to consist of two elements— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

- (a) one being an amount equal to his personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]
- (b) the other being an amount equal to the excess of the applicable amount over his personal rate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(8) Where a claimant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] is entitled to both a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance and an income-based conditions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] and has an income, the amount payable *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] by way of a jobseeker's allowance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b] shall be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

- (a) the amount by which the applicable amount exceeds his income, if the amount of that excess is greater than his personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]
- (b) his personal rate, if it is not. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(9) Where the amount payable to a claimant to whom paragraph (8) applies is the amount by which the applicable amount exceeds his income, the amount payable to him by way of a jobseeker's allowance shall be taken to consist of two elements— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

- (a) one being an amount equal to his personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]
- (b) the other being an amount equal to the amount by which the difference between the applicable amount and his income exceeds his personal rate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(10) The element of a jobseeker's allowance mentioned in paragraph (7)(a) and that mentioned in paragraph (9)(a) shall be treated, for the purpose of identifying the source of the allowance, as attributable to the claimant's entitlement to a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(11) The element of a jobseeker's allowance mentioned in paragraph (7)(b) and that mentioned in paragraph (9)(b) shall be treated, for the purpose of identifying the source of the allowance, as attributable to the claimant's entitlement to an income-based jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(11A) In paragraphs (6) to (11) “claimant” does not include— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

- (a) a joint-claim couple, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]
- (b) a member of such a couple (other than a person to whom regulations under Article 3(2C) apply); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

but Article 6A, which contains corresponding provisions relating to joint-claim couples, applies instead. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f42228dc3ea035ec6cdf4b316527b69b]

(12) Regulations under paragraph (5) may provide that, in prescribed cases, an applicable amount is to be nil.

#### 6A. Amount payable in respect of joint-claim couple[^c22545091]

(1)[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069] This Article applies where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) a joint-claim couple are entitled to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) one or each of the members of the couple is in addition entitled to a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

and in such a case the provisions of this Article have effect in relation to the couple in place of Article 6(3A). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(2) If a joint-claim couple falling within paragraph (1) have no income, the amount payable in respect of the couple by way of a jobseeker's allowance shall be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) the applicable amount, if that is greater than the couple's personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) the couple's personal rate, if it is not. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(3) Where the amount payable in accordance with paragraph (2) is the applicable amount, the amount payable in respect of the couple by way of a jobseeker's allowance shall be taken to consist of two elements— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) one being an amount equal to the couple's personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) the other being an amount equal to the excess of the applicable amount over the couple's personal rate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(4) Where the amount payable in accordance with paragraph (2) is the couple's personal rate, then— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) if each member of the couple is entitled to a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance, an amount equal to the member's own personal rate shall be payable in respect of the member by way of such an allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) if only one of them is so entitled, an amount equal to that member's personal rate shall be payable in respect of the member by way of such an allowance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

and in either case nothing shall be payable in respect of the couple by way of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(5) If a joint-claim couple falling within paragraph (1) have an income, the amount payable in respect of the couple by way of a jobseeker's allowance shall be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) the amount by which the applicable amount exceeds the couple's income, if the amount of that excess is greater than the couple's personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) the couple's personal rate, if it is not. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(6) Where the amount payable in accordance with paragraph (5) is the amount by which the applicable amount exceeds the couple's income, the amount payable in respect of the couple by way of a jobseeker's allowance shall be taken to consist of two elements— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) one being an amount equal to the couple's personal rate; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) the other being an amount equal to the amount by which the difference between the applicable amount and the couple's income exceeds the couple's personal rate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(7) Where the amount payable in accordance with paragraph (5) is the couple's personal rate, paragraph (4) shall apply as it applies in a case where the amount payable in accordance with paragraph (2) is that rate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(8) The element of a jobseeker's allowance mentioned in paragraph (3)(a) and that mentioned in paragraph (6)(a) shall be treated, for the purpose of identifying the source of the allowance, as attributable— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) in a case where only one member of the joint-claim couple is entitled to a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance, to that member's entitlement to such an allowance; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) in a case where each member of the couple is entitled to a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance, rateably according to their individual entitlements to such an allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(9) The element of a jobseeker's allowance mentioned in paragraph (3)(b) and that mentioned in paragraph (6)(b) shall be treated, for the purpose of identifying the source of the allowance, as attributable to the couple's entitlement to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

(10) In this Article “the couple's personal rate”, in relation to a joint-claim couple, means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

- (a) where only one member of the couple is entitled to a contribution-based jobseeker's allowance, that member's personal rate; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]
- (b) where each member of the couple is entitled to such an allowance, the aggregate of their personal rates. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-9e9f7436a387624d1b5f4fc3e1b51069]

#### 7. Duration of a contribution-based (affected by repeal, see note) jobseeker's allowance[^M_F_a2f09ec4-756c-47ab-ce44-0e93cb4bf46e]

(1) The period for which a person is entitled to a contribution-based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-cc20796857b80ac6ddd0f31a705903d2] jobseeker's allowance shall not exceed, in the aggregate, 182 days in any period for which his entitlement is established by reference (under Article 4(1)(b)) to the same two years.

(2) The fact that a person's entitlement to a contribution-based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a749ce8f21f68b91ac4509bb01c2c876] jobseeker's allowance ( “his previous entitlement”) has ceased as a result of paragraph (1) does not prevent his being entitled to a further contribution-based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a749ce8f21f68b91ac4509bb01c2c876] jobseeker's allowance if—

- (a) he satisfies the contribution-based conditions; and
- (b) the two years by reference to which he satisfies those conditions include at least one year which is later than the second of the two years by reference to which his previous entitlement was established.

(3) Regulations may provide that a person who would be entitled to a contribution-based *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-88db2f16050410f1fd89b2be02332e62] jobseeker's allowance but for the operation of prescribed provisions of, or made under, this Order shall be treated as if entitled to the allowance for the purposes of this Article.

### Work-related requirements[^c-649f266064b8851b47e7e401941ee6d1]

#### 8. Work-related requirements

(1) The following provisions provide for the Department to impose work-related requirements with which claimants must comply for the purposes of this Order.

(2) In this Order “**work-related requirement**” means—

- (a) a work-focused interview requirement (see Article 8B);
- (b) a work preparation requirement (see Article 8C);
- (c) a work search requirement (see Article 8D);
- (d) a work availability requirement (see Article 8E).

#### 8A. Claimant commitment

(1) A claimant commitment is a record of a claimant's responsibilities in relation to an award of a jobseeker's allowance.

(2) A claimant commitment is to be prepared by the Department and may be reviewed and updated as the Department thinks fit.

(3) A claimant commitment is to be in such form as the Department thinks fit.

(4) A claimant commitment is to include—

- (a) a record of the requirements that the claimant must comply with under this Order (or such of them as the Department considers it appropriate to include),
- (b) any prescribed information, and
- (c) any other information the Department considers it appropriate to include.

(5) For the purposes of this Order a claimant accepts a claimant commitment if, and only if, the claimant accepts the most up-to-date version of it in such manner as may be prescribed.

#### 8B. Work-focused interview requirement

(1) In this Order a “work-focused interview requirement” is a requirement that a claimant participate in one or more work-focused interviews as specified by the Department.

(2) A work-focused interview is an interview for prescribed purposes relating to work or work preparation.

(3) The purposes which may be prescribed under paragraph (2) include in particular that of making it more likely in the opinion of the Department that the claimant will obtain paid work (or more paid work or better-paid work).

(4) The Department may specify how, when and where a work-focused interview is to take place.

#### 8C. Work preparation requirement

(1) In this Order a “work preparation requirement” is a requirement that a claimant take particular action specified by the Department for the purpose of making it more likely in the opinion of the Department that the claimant will obtain paid work (or more paid work or better-paid work).

(2) The Department may under paragraph (1) specify the time to be devoted to any particular action.

(3) Action which may be specified under paragraph (1) includes in particular—

- (a) attending a skills assessment;
- (b) improving personal presentation;
- (c) participating in training;
- (d) participating in an employment programme;
- (e) undertaking work experience or a work placement;
- (f) developing a business plan;
- (g) any action prescribed for the purpose in paragraph (1).

#### 8D. Work search requirement

(1) In this Order a “work search requirement” is a requirement that a claimant take—

- (a) all reasonable action, and
- (b) any particular action specified by the Department,

for the purpose of obtaining paid work (or more paid work or better-paid work).

(2) The Department may under paragraph (1)(b) specify the time to be devoted to any particular action.

(3) Action which may be specified under paragraph (1)(b) includes in particular—

- (a) carrying out work searches;
- (b) making applications;
- (c) creating and maintaining an online profile;
- (d) registering with an employment agency;
- (e) seeking references;
- (f) any other action prescribed for the purpose in paragraph (1).

(4) Regulations may impose limitations on a work search requirement by reference to the work to which it relates; and the Department may in any particular case specify further such limitations on such a requirement.

(5) A limitation under paragraph (4) may in particular be by reference to—

- (a) work of a particular nature,
- (b) work with a particular level of remuneration,
- (c) work in particular locations, or
- (d) work available for a certain number of hours per week or at particular times,

and may be indefinite or for a particular period.

#### 8E. Work availability requirement

(1) In this Order a “work availability requirement” is a requirement that a claimant be available for work.

(2) For the purposes of this Article “**available for work**” means able and willing immediately to take up paid work (or more paid work or better-paid work).

(3) Regulations may impose limitations on a work availability requirement by reference to the work to which it relates; and the Department may in any particular case specify further such limitations on such a requirement.

(4) A limitation under paragraph (3) may in particular be by reference to—

- (a) work of a particular nature,
- (b) work with a particular level of remuneration,
- (c) work in particular locations, or
- (d) work available for a certain number of hours per week or at particular times,

and may be indefinite or for a particular period.

(5) Regulations may for the purposes of paragraph (2) define what is meant by able and willing immediately to take up work.

#### 8F. Imposition of work-related requirements

(1) The Department must, except in prescribed circumstances, impose on a claimant—

- (a) a work search requirement, and
- (b) a work availability requirement.

(2) The Department may, subject to this Order, impose either or both of the following on a claimant—

- (a) a work-focused interview requirement;
- (b) a work preparation requirement.

#### 8G. Connected requirements

(1) The Department may require a claimant to participate in an interview for any purpose relating to—

- (a) the imposition of a work-related requirement on the claimant;
- (b) verifying the claimant's compliance with a work-related requirement;
- (c) assisting the claimant to comply with a work-related requirement.

(2) The Department may specify how, when and where such an interview is to take place.

(3) The Department may, for the purpose of verifying the claimant's compliance with a work-related requirement, require a claimant to—

- (a) provide to the Department information and evidence specified by the Department in a manner so specified;
- (b) confirm compliance in a manner so specified.

(4) The Department may require a claimant to report to the Department any specified changes in the claimant's circumstances which are relevant to—

- (a) the imposition of work-related requirements on the claimant;
- (b) the claimant's compliance with a work-related requirement.

#### 8H. Imposition of work-related and connected requirements: supplementary

(1) Regulations may make provision—

- (a) where the Department may impose a requirement under the preceding provisions, as to when the requirement must or must not be imposed;
- (b) where the Department may specify any action to be taken in relation to a requirement under the preceding provisions, as to what action must or must not be specified;
- (c) where the Department may specify any other matter in relation to such a requirement, as to what must or must not be specified in respect of that matter.

(2) Where the Department may impose a work-focused interview requirement, the Department must have regard to such matters as may be prescribed.

(3) Where the Department may specify a particular action under Article 8C(1) or 8D(1)(b), the Department must have regard to such matters as may be prescribed.

(4) Where the Department may impose a requirement under the preceding provisions, the Department may revoke or change what has been imposed or specified.

(5) Where the Department may specify any action to be taken in relation to a requirement imposed under the preceding provisions, the Department may revoke or change what has been specified.

(6) Notification of a requirement imposed under the preceding provisions (or any change to or revocation of such a requirement) is, if not included in the claimant commitment, to be in such manner as the Department may determine.

(7) Regulations must make provision to secure that, in prescribed circumstances, where a claimant has recently been a victim of domestic violence—

- (a) a requirement imposed on the claimant under the preceding provisions ceases to have effect for a period of 13 weeks, and
- (b) the Department may not impose any other requirement on the claimant during that period.

(8) For the purposes of paragraph (7)—

- (a) “domestic violence” has such meaning as may be prescribed;
- (b) “**victim of domestic violence**” means a person on or against whom domestic violence is inflicted or threatened (and regulations under paragraph (7) may prescribe circumstances in which a person is to be treated as being or not being a victim of domestic violence);
- (c) a person has recently been a victim of domestic violence if a prescribed period has not expired since the violence was inflicted or threatened.

#### 8I. Compliance with work-related and connected requirements

Regulations may make provision as to circumstances in which a claimant is to be treated as having—

- (a) complied with or not complied with any requirement imposed under the preceding provisions or any aspect of such a requirement, or
- (b) taken or not taken any particular action specified by the Department in relation to such a requirement.

#### 8J. Higher-level sanctions

(1) The amount of an award of jobseeker's allowance is to be reduced in accordance with this Article in the event of a failure by a claimant which is sanctionable under this Article.

(2) It is a failure sanctionable under this Article if a claimant—

- (a) fails for no good reason to comply with a requirement imposed under a work preparation requirement to undertake a work placement of a prescribed description;
- (b) fails for no good reason to comply with a requirement imposed under a work search requirement to apply for a particular vacancy for paid work;
- (c) fails for no good reason to comply with a work availability requirement by not taking up an offer of paid work;
- (d) by reason of misconduct, or voluntarily and for no good reason, ceases paid work or loses pay.

(3) It is a failure sanctionable under this Article if, at any time before making the claim by reference to which the award is made, the claimant—

- (a) for no good reason failed to take up an offer of paid work, or
- (b) by reason of misconduct, or voluntarily and for no good reason, ceased paid work or lost pay.

(4) For the purposes of paragraphs (2) and (3) regulations may provide—

- (a) for circumstances in which ceasing to work or losing pay is to be treated as occurring or not occurring by reason of misconduct or voluntarily;
- (b) for loss of pay below a prescribed level to be disregarded.

(5) Regulations are to specify—

- (a) the amount of a reduction under this Article;
- (b) the period for which such a reduction has effect, not exceeding 18 months in relation to any failure sanctionable under this Article.

(6) Regulations under paragraph (5)(b) may in particular provide for the period of a reduction to depend on either or both of the following—

- (a) the number of failures by the claimant sanctionable under this Article;
- (b) the period between such failures.

(7) Regulations may provide—

- (a) for cases in which no reduction is to be made under this Article;
- (b) for a reduction under this Article made in relation to an award that is terminated to be applied to any new award made within a prescribed period of the termination;
- (c) for the termination or suspension of a reduction under this Article.

#### 8K. Other sanctions

(1) The amount of an award of a jobseeker's allowance is to be reduced in accordance with this Article in the event of a failure by a claimant which is sanctionable under this Article.

(2) It is a failure sanctionable under this Article if a claimant—

- (a) fails for no good reason to comply with a work-related requirement;
- (b) fails for no good reason to comply with a requirement under Article 8G.

(3) But a failure by a claimant is not sanctionable under this Article if it is also a failure sanctionable under Article 8J.

(4) Regulations must specify—

- (a) the amount of a reduction under this Article;
- (b) the period for which such a reduction has effect.

(5) Regulations under paragraph (4)(b) may provide that a reduction under this Article in relation to any failure is to have effect for—

- (a) a period continuing until the claimant meets a compliance condition specified by the Department,
- (b) a fixed period not exceeding 26 weeks which is—
  - (i) specified in the regulations, or
  - (ii) determined in any case by the Department, or
- (c) a combination of both.

(6) In paragraph (5)(a) “**compliance condition**” means—

- (a) a condition that the failure ceases, or
- (b) a condition relating to future compliance with a work-related requirement or a requirement under Article 8G.

(7) A compliance condition specified under paragraph (5)(a) may be—

- (a) revoked or varied by the Department;
- (b) notified to the claimant in such manner as the Department may determine.

(8) A period fixed under paragraph (5)(b) may in particular depend on either or both the following—

- (a) the number of failures by the claimant sanctionable under this Article;
- (b) the period between such failures.

(9) Regulations may provide—

- (a) for cases in which no reduction is to be made under this Article;
- (b) for a reduction under this Article made in relation to an award that is terminated to be applied to any new award made within a prescribed period of the termination;
- (c) for the termination or suspension of a reduction under this Article.

#### 8L. Concurrent exercise of certain functions by Department for Employment and Learning[^c-934ebdf3b4a389af823e81004b970322]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### 8M. Delegation and contracting out

(1) The functions of the Department under Articles 8 to 8I may be exercised by, or by the employees of, such person as the Department may authorise for the purpose (an “authorised person”).

(2) An authorisation given by virtue of this Article may authorise the exercise of a function—

- (a) wholly or to a limited extent;
- (b) generally or in particular cases or areas;
- (c) unconditionally or subject to conditions.

(3) An authorisation under this Article—

- (a) may specify its duration;
- (b) may be varied or revoked at any time by the Department;
- (c) does not prevent the Department or another person from exercising the function to which the authorisation relates.

(4) Anything done or omitted to be done by or in relation to an authorised person (or an employee of that person) in, or in connection with, the exercise or purported exercise of the function concerned is to be treated for all purposes as done or omitted to be done by or in relation to the Department or (as the case may be) an officer of the Department.

(5) Paragraph (4) does not apply—

- (a) for the purposes of so much of any contract made between the authorised person and the Department as relates to the exercise of the function, or
- (b) for the purposes of any criminal proceedings brought in respect of anything done or omitted to be done by the authorised person (or an employee of that person).

(6) Where—

- (a) the authorisation of an authorised person is revoked, and
- (b) at the time of the revocation so much of any contract made between the authorised person and the Department as relates to the exercise of the function is subsisting,

the authorised person is entitled to treat the contract as repudiated by the Department (and not as frustrated by reason of the revocation).

(7)[^c-16179b596d662dae18563909171dc694] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### 9. Actively seeking employment

(1)[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0] For the purposes of this Order, a person is actively seeking employment in any week if he takes in that week such steps as he can reasonably be expected to have to take in order to have the best prospects of securing employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(2) Regulations may make provision— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

- (a) with respect to steps which it is reasonable, for the purposes of paragraph (1), for a person to be expected to have to take in any week; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]
- (b) as to circumstances (for example, his skills, qualifications, abilities and physical or mental limitations) which, in particular, are to be taken into account in determining whether, in relation to any steps taken by a person, the requirements of paragraph (1) are satisfied in any week. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(3) Regulations may make provision for acts of a person which would otherwise be relevant for purposes of this Article to be disregarded in such circumstances (including circumstances constituted by, or connected with, his behaviour or appearance) as may be prescribed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(4) Regulations may prescribe circumstances in which, for the purposes of this Order, a person is to be treated as actively seeking employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(5) Regulations under paragraph (4) may, in particular, provide for a person who is actively seeking employment— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

- (a) only in his usual occupation, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]
- (b) only at a level of remuneration not lower than that which he is accustomed to receive, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]
- (c) only in his usual occupation and at a level of remuneration not lower than that which he is accustomed to receive, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

to be treated, for the permitted period determined in his case for the purposes of Article 8(5), as actively seeking employment during that period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(6) Regulations may provide for this Article, and any regulations made under it, to have effect in relation to a person who has reached the age of 16 but not the age of 18 as if “employment” included “training”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(7) Where it has been determined *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0][^c21802041] ( “the first determination”) that a person is to be treated, for the purposes of this Order, as actively seeking employment in any week, the question whether he is actively seeking employment in that week may subsequently be determined *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0][^c21802051] on a review of the first determination. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

(8) For the purposes of this Article— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

- “employment” means employed earner's employment or, in prescribed circumstances— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]
  - (a) self-employed earner's employment; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]
  - (b) employed earner's employment and self-employed earner's employment; and “employed earner's employment” and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]
- “self-employed earner's employment” have the same meanings as in the Benefits Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_0e32b84f-13de-4db9-e38f-8af91fc6e6f0]

#### 10. Attendance, information and evidence

(1)[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] Regulations may make provision for requiring a claimant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] (other than a joint-claim couple claiming a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance)[^c16984231] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]

- (a) To *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] participate in an interview in such manner, time and place[^c-278f9e0a40eec4fd4c174c485abf311f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] as *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] an employment officer *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] may specify; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]
- (b) to provide information and such evidence as may be prescribed as to his circumstances, his availability for employment and the extent to which he is actively seeking employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]

(1A) Regulations may make provision— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]

- (a) for requiring each member of a joint-claim couple claiming a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] participate in an interview in such manner, time and place[^c-69f2bea48cd5f00d87fd8cd17000a6a0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] as *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] an employment officer[^c-6892e5cd604d33c7f25d83088b20f9cf] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] , may specify; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]
- (b) for requiring a member of such a couple to provide information and such evidence as may be prescribed as to his circumstances, his availability for employment and the extent to which he is actively seeking employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]
- (c) for requiring such a couple to jointly provide information and such evidence as may be prescribed as to the circumstances of each or either member of the couple, the availability for employment of each or either member of the couple and the extent to which each or either member of the couple is actively seeking employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]
- (d) where any requirement to provide information or evidence is imposed on such a couple by virtue of sub-paragraph (c), for the joint obligation of the couple to be capable of being discharged by the provision of the information or evidence by one member of the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]

(2)[^c22546041] Regulations under paragraph (1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] or (1A) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] may, in particular— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]

- (a)[^c-553aa80c0bdd9c8be21e2f62a21521ef] provide for entitlement to a jobseeker's allowance to cease at such time as may be determined in accordance with any such regulations if, when a person fails to comply with such regulations, that person (or, if that person is a member of a joint-claim couple, either member of the couple) does not make prescribed contact with an employment officer within a prescribed period of the failure; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66]
- (ca (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-6df6b122b1cd941114845c1b77414865][^M_F_7388b09d-ecbd-46d3-9cc1-dd004bad9d66] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (d)[^c-6df6b122b1cd941114845c1b77414865] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(2A)[^c-fcf317fdd5e7de51f79b8673637e1759] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(3)[^c-fcf317fdd5e7de51f79b8673637e1759] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### 11. The jobseeker's agreement

(1)[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4] An agreement which is entered into by a claimant and an employment officer and which complies with the prescribed requirements in force at the time when the agreement is made is referred to in this Order as “a jobseeker's agreement”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(2) A jobseeker's agreement shall have effect only for the purposes of Article 3. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(3) A jobseeker's agreement shall be in writing and be signed by both parties. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(3A)[^c22546411] The agreement may be in electronic form and signed by means of an electronic signature (within the meaning given in section 7(2) of the Electronic Communications Act 2000 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4] [^c22546431]).

(4) A copy of the agreement shall be given to the claimant. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(5) An employment officer shall not enter into a jobseeker's agreement with a claimant unless, in the officer's opinion, the conditions mentioned in Article 3(2)(a) and (c) would be satisfied with respect to the claimant if he were to comply with, or be treated as complying with, the proposed agreement. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(6) The employment officer may, and if asked to do so by the claimant shall forthwith, refer a proposed jobseeker's agreement to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4][^c21802091] an adjudication officer for him to determine— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

- (a) whether, if the claimant concerned were to comply with the proposed agreement, he would satisfy— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
  - (i) the condition mentioned in Article 3(2)(a), or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4] the condition mentioned in Article 3(2)(c); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
- (b) whether it is reasonable to expect the claimant to have to comply with the proposed agreement. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(7) [^c21802091]An adjudication officer to whom a reference is made under paragraph (6)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

- (a) shall so far as practicable dispose of it in accordance with this Article before the end of the period of 14 days from the date of the reference; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
- (b) may give such directions, with respect to the terms on which the employment officer is to enter into a jobseeker's agreement with the claimant, as *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4][^c21802091] the adjudication officer considers appropriate; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
- (c) may direct that, if such conditions as *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4][^c21802091] he considers appropriate are satisfied, the proposed jobseeker's agreement is to be treated (if entered into) as having effect on such date, before it would otherwise have effect, as may be specified in the direction. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(8) Regulations may provide— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

- (a) for such matters as may be prescribed to be taken into account by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4][^c21802091] an adjudication officer in giving a direction under paragraph (7)(c); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
- (b) for such persons as may be prescribed to be notified of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
  - (i) any determination of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4][^c21802091] an adjudication officer under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4] any direction given by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4][^c21802091] an adjudication officer under this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(9)[^c21802101] Any determination of adjudication officer under this Article shall be binding. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(10) Regulations may provide that, in prescribed circumstances, a claimant is to be treated as having satisfied the condition mentioned in Article 3(2)(b). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(11) Regulations may provide that, in prescribed circumstances, a jobseeker's agreement is to be treated as having effect on a date, to be determined in accordance with the regulations, before it would otherwise have effect. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

(12) Except in such circumstances as may be prescribed, a jobseeker's agreement entered into by a claimant shall cease to have effect on the coming to an end of an award of a jobseeker's allowance made to him *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4] or to a joint-claim couple of which he is a member.[^c16984491] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4].

(13) In this Article and Article 12 “employment officer” means an officer of the Department or such other person as may be designated for the purposes of this Article by an order made by the Department. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_23c4f997-c5ef-407c-d957-d7c8e113b5c4]

#### 12. Variation of jobseeker's agreement

(1)[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773] A jobseeker's agreement may be varied, in the prescribed manner, by agreement between the claimant and any employment officer. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(2) Any agreement to vary a jobseeker's agreement shall be in writing and be signed by both parties. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(2A)[^c22546461] Any agreement to vary a jobseeker's agreement may be in electronic form and signed by means of an electronic signature (within the meaning given in section 7(2) of the Electronic Communications Act 2000). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(3) A copy of the agreement, as varied, shall be given to the claimant. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(4) An employment officer shall not agree to a variation of a jobseeker's agreement, unless, in the officer's opinion, the conditions mentioned in Article 3(2)(a) and (c) would continue to be satisfied with respect to the claimant if he were to comply with, or be treated as complying with, the agreement as proposed to be varied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(5) The employment officer may, and if asked to do so by the claimant shall forthwith, refer a proposed variation of a jobseeker's agreement to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773][^c21802131] an adjudication officer for him to determine— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

- (a) whether, if the claimant concerned were to comply with the agreement as proposed to be varied, he would satisfy— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (i) the condition mentioned in Article 3(2)(a), or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773] the condition mentioned in Article 3(2)(c); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
- (b) whether it is reasonable to expect the claimant to have to comply with the agreement as proposed to be varied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(6) [^c21802131]An adjudication officer to whom a reference is made under paragraph (5)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

- (a) shall so far as practicable dispose of it in accordance with this Article before the end of the period of 14 days from the date of the reference; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
- (b) shall give such directions as *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773][^c21802131] he considers appropriate as to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (i) whether the jobseeker's agreement should be varied, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773] if so, the terms on which the claimant and the employment officer are to enter into an agreement to vary it; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
- (c) may bring the jobseeker's agreement to an end where the claimant fails, within a prescribed period, to comply with a direction given under sub-paragraph (b)(ii); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
- (d) may direct that, if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (i) the jobseeker's agreement is varied, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773] such conditions as *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773][^c21802131] he considers appropriate are satisfied, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

  the agreement as varied is to be treated as having effect on such date, before it would otherwise have effect, as may be specified in the direction. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(7) Regulations may provide— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

- (a) or such matters as may be prescribed to be taken into account by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773][^c21802131] an adjudication officer in giving a direction under paragraph (6)(b) or (d); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
- (b) for such persons as may be prescribed to be notified of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (i) any determination of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773][^c21802131] an adjudication officer under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773] any direction given by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773][^c21802131] an adjudication officer under this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

(8)[^c21802141] Any determination of an adjudication officer under this Article shall be binding. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_5d372dea-f97b-45d3-e98d-d9d7cf144773]

#### 13. Jobseeker's agreement: reviews and appeals[^c21802151]

(1) Any determination of, or direction given by, an adjudication officer under Article 11 or 12 may be reviewed (by a different adjudication officer) on the application of the claimant or of an employment officer.

(2) Regulations may make provision with respect to the procedure to be followed on a review under this Article.

(3) The claimant may appeal to a social security appeal tribunal against any determination of, or direction given by, an adjudication officer on a review under this Article.

(4) A social security appeal tribunal determining an appeal under this Article may give a direction of a kind which an adjudication officer may give under Article 11(7)(b) or (c) or (as the case may be) Article 12(6)(b) or (d).

(5) Where a social security appeal tribunal gives a direction under paragraph (4) of a kind which may be given by an adjudication officer under Article 12(6)(b)(ii), an adjudication officer may bring the job seeker's agreement to an end if the claimant fails to comply with the direction within a prescribed period.

(6) An appropriate person may, on the ground that it was erroneous in point of law, appeal to a Commissioner against the decision of a social security appeal tribunal on an appeal under this Article.

(7) Any of the following is an appropriate person for the purposes of paragraph (6)—

- (a) the claimant;
- (b) an adjudication officer;
- (c) in prescribed circumstances, a trade union;
- (d) in prescribed circumstances, any other association which exists to promote the interests and welfare of its members.

(8) Subsections (7) to (10) of section 21 of the Administration Act (appeals to Commissioners) shall apply in relation to appeals under this Article as they apply in relation to appeals under that section.

(9) In this Article "Commissioner" has the same meaning as in the Administration Act.

### Income and Capital

#### 14. Income and capital: general

(1) In relation to a claim for a jobseeker's allowance, the income and capital of a person shall be calculated or estimated in such manner as may be prescribed.

(2) A person's income in respect of a week shall be calculated in accordance with prescribed rules.

(3) The rules may provide for the calculation to be made by reference to an average over a period (which need not include the week concerned).

(4) Circumstances may be prescribed in which—

- (a) a person is treated as possessing capital or income which he does not possess;
- (b) capital or income which a person does possess is to be disregarded;
- (c) income is to be treated as capital;
- (d) capital is to be treated as income;

#### 15. Income and capital: income-based jobseeker's allowance

(1)[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c] No person shall be entitled to an income-based jobseeker's allowance if his capital, or a prescribed part of it, exceeds the prescribed amount. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]
