(2) Where a person claiming an income-based jobseeker's allowance is a member of a family, the income and capital of any member of that family shall, except in prescribed circumstances, be treated as the income and capital of the claimant. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]

(2A)[^c16984601] Paragraphs (1) and (2) do not apply as regards a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance; but a joint-claim couple shall not be entitled to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance if the couple's capital, or a prescribed part of it, exceeds the prescribed amount. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]

(2B) Where a joint-claim couple claim a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]

- (a) the couple's income and capital includes the separate income and capital of each of them; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]
- (b) the income and capital of any other person who is a member of any family of which the couple are members shall, except in prescribed circumstances, be treated as income and capital of the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]

(3) Regulations may provide that capital not exceeding the amount prescribed under paragraph (1) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c] or (2A) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c], but exceeding a prescribed lower amount, shall be treated, to a prescribed extent, as if it were income of a prescribed amount. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a0c2d88cdf71e6df959a7a0cf6119b6c]

### Trade disputes

#### 16. Trade disputes

(1) Where—

- (a) there is a stoppage of work which causes a person not to be employed on any day, and
- (b) the stoppage is due to a trade dispute at his place of work,

that person, is not entitled to a jobseeker's allowance for the week which includes that day unless he proves that he is not directly interested in the dispute.

(2) A person who withdraws his labour on any day in furtherance of a trade dispute, but to whom paragraph (1) does not apply, is not entitled to a jobseeker's allowance for the week which includes that day.

(3) If a person who is prevented by paragraph (1) from being entitled to a jobseeker's allowance proves that during the stoppage—

- (a) he became bona fide employed elsewhere;
- (b) his employment was terminated by reason of redundancy within the meaning of Article 174(1) of the Employment Rights (Northern Ireland) Order 1996[^c16984661], or
- (c) he bona fide resumed employment with his employer but subsequently left for a reason other than the trade dispute,

paragraph (1) shall be taken to have ceased to apply to him on the occurrence of the event referred to in sub-paragraph (a) or (b) or (as the case may be) the first event referred to in sub-paragraph (c).

(4) In this Article “place of work”, in relation to any person, means the premises or place at which he was employed.

(5) Where separate branches of work which are commonly carried on as separate businesses in separate premises or at separate places are in any case carried on in separate departments on the same premises or at the same place, each of those departments shall, for the purposes of paragraph (4), be deemed to be separate premises or (as the case may be) a separate place.

#### 17. Effect on other claimants

(1)[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] Except in prescribed circumstances, paragraph (2) applies in relation to a claimant for an income-based jobseeker's allowance where a member of his family ( “A”) is, or would be, prevented by Article 16 from being entitled to a jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(2) For the purposes of calculating the claimant's entitlement to an income-based jobseeker's allowance— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) any portion of the applicable amount which is included in respect of A shall be disregarded for the period for which this paragraph applies to the claimant; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) where the claimant and A are a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] couple[^c16984681] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802], any portion of the applicable amount which is included in respect of them shall be reduced to one half for the period for which this paragraph applies to the claimant; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (c) except so far as regulations provide otherwise, there shall be treated as the claimant's income— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (i) any amount which becomes, or would on an application duly made become, available to A in relation to that period by way of repayment of income tax deducted from A's *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] taxable earnings (as defined by section 10 of the Income Tax (Earnings and Pensions) Act 2003 under regulations made under section 684 of that Act ([^c16984701] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]PAYE *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] regulations) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] any other payment which the claimant or any member of his family receives or is entitled to obtain because A is without employment for that period; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (d) any payment by way of a jobseeker's allowance for that period or any part of it which apart from this sub-paragraph would be made to the claimant— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (i) shall not be made, if the weekly rate of payment ( “the rate”) would be equal to or less than the prescribed sum; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] shall be at a weekly rate equal to the difference between the rate and the prescribed sum, if the rate would be more than the prescribed sum. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(3) Where a reduction under paragraph (2)(b) would not produce a sum which is a multiple of 5p, the reduction shall be to the nearest lower sum which is such a multiple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(4) Where A returns to work with the same employer after a period during which paragraph (2) applied to the claimant (whether or not his return is before the end of any stoppage of work in relation to which he is, or would be, prevented from being entitled to a jobseeker's allowance), paragraph (2) shall cease to apply to the claimant at the commencement of the day on which A returns to work. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(5) In relation to any period of less than a week, paragraph (2) shall have effect subject to such modifications as may be prescribed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(6) Paragraphs (7) to (9) apply where an order made under section 132 of the Administration Act (annual up-rating of benefits) has the effect of increasing the sum prescribed in regulations made under Article 6(5) as the personal allowance for a single person aged not less than 25 ( “the personal allowance”). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(7) For the sum prescribed in regulations made under paragraph (2)(d) there shall be substituted, from the time when the order comes into operation, a sum arrived at by increasing the prescribed sum by the percentage by which the personal allowance has been increase by the order. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(8) If the sum arrived at under paragraph (7) is not a multiple of 50p— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) any remainder of 25p or less shall be disregarded; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) any remainder of more than 25p shall be rounded up to the nearest 50p. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(9) The order shall state the sum substituted for the sum prescribed in regulations made under paragraph (2)(d). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(10) Nothing in paragraph (7) prevents the making of further regulations under paragraph (2)(d) varying the prescribed sum. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

#### 17A. Trade disputes: joint-claim couples[^c22545111]

(1) Articles 16 and 17 shall, in relation to a joint-claim couple claiming a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, apply in accordance with this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(2) Where each member of the couple is prevented by Article 16 from being entitled to a jobseeker's allowance, the couple are not entitled to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(3) But where only one member of the couple is prevented by that Article from being entitled to a jobseeker's allowance, the couple are not for that reason alone prevented from being entitled to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(4) Article 17(1) does not have effect in relation to the couple but, except in prescribed circumstances, Article 17(2) applies for the purposes of calculating the couple's entitlement to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) a member of the couple, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) any other person who is a member of any family of which the couple are members, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

is, or would be, prevented by Article 16 from being entitled to a jobseeker's allowance. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(5) Where Article 17(2) applies in relation to the couple by virtue of paragraph (4), that provision and Article 17(4) apply with the following modifications— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) references to the claimant are to be taken as references to the couple; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) references to “A” are to the person mentioned in paragraph (4)(a) or (b); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (c) Article 17(2)(b) has effect as if for “where the claimant and A are a *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] couple[^c16984721] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802],” there were substituted “where A is a member of the couple,”; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (d) Article 17(2)(c)(ii) has effect as if for “of his family” there were substituted “of any family of which the couple are members”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

### Persons under 18

#### 18. Severe hardship

(1) If it appears to the Department— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) that a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (i) has reached the age of 16 but not the age of 18, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] is not entitled to a jobseeker's allowance or to income support, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] is registered for training but is not being provided with any training, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) that severe hardship will result to him unless a jobseeker's allowance is paid to him, the Department may direct that this Article is to apply to him. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(2) A direction may be given so as to have effect for a specified period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(3) The Department may revoke a direction if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) it appears to the Department that there has been a change of circumstances as a result of which failure to receive a jobseeker's allowance need no longer result in severe hardship to the person concerned; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b)[^c21802161] it appears to the Department that the person concerned— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (i) failed to pursue an opportunity of obtaining training, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] rejected an offer of training, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

and has not shown good reason for doing so; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (c) the Department is satisfied that it was given in ignorance of some material fact or was based on a mistake as to some material fact and considers that, but for the ignorance or mistake, the Department would not have given the direction. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(4)[^c21802161] In this Article— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

“period” includes— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) a period of a determinate length; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) a period defined by reference to the happening of a future event; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (c) a period of a determinate length but subject to earlier determination upon the happening of a future event. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

#### 19. Reduced payments

(1) Regulations may provide for the amount of an income-based jobseeker's allowance *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] payable in respect of[^c16984761] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] any young person to whom this Article applies to be reduced— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) in such circumstances, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) by such a percentage, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (c) for such a period, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

as may be prescribed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(1A) Regulations may provide for the amount of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance payable in respect of any joint-claim couple where a member of the couple is a young person to whom this Article applies to be reduced— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) in such circumstances, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) by such a percentage, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (c) for such a period, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

as may be prescribed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(2) This Article applies to any young person in respect of whom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) a direction is in force under Article 18; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) [^c21802171]either of the conditions mentioned in paragraph (3) is satisfied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(3) The conditions are that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) the young person was previously entitled to an income-based jobseeker's allowance and that entitlement ceased by virtue of the revocation of a direction under Article 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b)[^c21802171] he has failed to complete a course of training and no certificate has been issued to him under paragraph (4) with respect to that failure. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(4)[^c21802171] Where a young person who has failed to complete a course of training— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

- (a) claims that there was *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] a good reason[^c-1d98f095aa9314258ad1c7ca56ed097c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] for the failure, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]
- (b) applies to the Department for a certificate under this paragraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

the Department shall, if it is satisfied that there was *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] a good reason *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802] for the failure, issue a certificate to that effect and give a copy of it to the young person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

(5)[^c21802171] In this Article “young person” means a person who has reached the age of 16 but not the age of 18. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-3dd2359640b45e7446bceff458a17802]

#### 20. Recovery of overpayments

**No longer in force (ended 2010-08-13).**

In the Administration Act, insert after section 69—

> **Jobseeker's allowance**
>
> **69A. Recovery of jobseeker's allowance: severe hardship cases.**
>
> (1) Where—
>
> - (a) a severe hardship direction is revoked; and
> - (b) it is determined by an adjudication officer that—
>   - (i) whether fraudulently or otherwise, any person has misrepresented, or failed to disclose, any material fact; and
>   - (ii) in consequence of the failure of misrepresentation, payment of a jobseeker's allowance has been made during the relevant period to the person to whom the direction related,
>
>   an adjudication officer may determine that the Department is entitled to recover the amount of the payment.
>
> (2) In this section—
>
> - “severe hardship direction” means a direction given under Article 18 of the Jobseekers (Northern Ireland) Order 1995; and
> - “the relevant period” means—
>   - (a) if the revocation is under Article 18(3)(a) of that Order, the period beginning with the date of the change of circumstances and ending with the date of the revocation; and
>   - (b) if the revocation is under Article 18(3)(b) or (c) of that Order, the period during which the direction was in force.
>
> (3) Where a severe hardship direction is revoked, the Department may certify whether there has been misrepresentation of a material fact or failure to disclose a material fact.
>
> (4) If the Department certifies that there has been such misrepresentation or failure to disclose, it may certify—
>
> - (a) who made the misrepresentation or failed to make the disclosure; and
> - (b) whether or not a payment of jobseeker's allowance has been made in consequence of the misrepresentation or failure.
>
> (5) If the Department certifies that a payment has been made, it may certify the period during which a jobseeker's allowance would not have been paid but for the misrepresentation or failure to disclose.
>
> (6) A certificate under this section shall be conclusive as to any matter certified.
>
> (7) Subsections (3) and (6) to (10) of section 69 above apply to a jobseeker's allowance recoverable under subsection (1) above as they apply to a jobseeker's allowance recoverable under section 69(1) above.
>
> (8) The other provisions of section 69 above do not apply to a jobseeker's allowance recoverable under subsection (1) above.

.

### “Work for your benefit” schemes etc.[^c22545031]

#### 19A. Schemes for assisting persons to obtain employment: “work for your benefit” schemes etc.

(1)[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b] Regulations may make provision for or in connection with imposing on claimants in prescribed circumstances a requirement to participate in schemes of any prescribed description that are designed to assist them to obtain employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

(2) Regulations under this Article may, in particular, require participants to undertake work, or work-related activity, during any prescribed period with a view to improving their prospects of obtaining employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

(3) In paragraph (2) “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]**work-related activity *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]**”, in relation to any person, means activity which makes it more likely that the person will obtain or remain in work or be able to do so *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

(4) Regulations under this Article may not require a person to participate in a scheme unless the person would (apart from the regulations) be required to meet the jobseeking conditions. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

(5) Regulations under this Article may, in particular, make provision— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

- (a) for notifying participants of the requirement to participate in a scheme within paragraph (1); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]
- (b) for securing that participants are not required to meet the jobseeking conditions or are not required to meet such of those conditions as are specified in the regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]
- (c) for suspending any jobseeker's agreement to which a person is a party for any period during which the person is a participant; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]
- (d)[^c-14f973abc80dfc06e41311b07fd817d4] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
- (e)[^c-14f973abc80dfc06e41311b07fd817d4] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(9)[^c-99dbfe1b5973b2ded28d9d5c26c84a61] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(10) In this Article— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

- “[^c-5212671812c3301b213a2d051ba46440] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]**claimant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5212671812c3301b213a2d051ba46440] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]**”, in relation to a joint-claim couple claiming a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, means either or both of the members of the couple; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-5212671812c3301b213a2d051ba46440] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]
- “**the jobseeking conditions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]**” means the conditions set out in Article 3(2)(a) to (c); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]
- “**participant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]**”, in relation to any time, means any person who is required at that time to participate in a scheme within paragraph (1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-73793132b6423c0399d8ecf96273180b]

#### 19B. Article 19A: supplemental

(1)[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef] For the purposes of, or in connection with, any scheme within Article 19A(1) the Department *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef] [^c-ccfaf93d4d28b12ba600a0db0741d7f9]... may— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

- (a) make arrangements (whether or not with other persons) for the provision of facilities; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

- (c) make payments (by way of fees, grants, loans or otherwise) to persons undertaking the provision of facilities under arrangements within sub-paragraph (a) or (b); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]
- (d) make payments (by way of grants, loans or otherwise) to persons participating in the scheme; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]
- (e) make payments in respect of incidental expenses. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

(2) In paragraph (1) “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]**facilities *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]**” includes services, and any reference to the provision of facilities includes the making of payments to persons participating in the scheme. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

(3) The power of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef] the Department for Communities or the Department for the Economy[^c-8f63807a81a7a10574ed2dc0d03a08b7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef] to make an order under Article 4 of the Employment and Training (Amendment) (Northern Ireland) Order 1988 (status of trainees) includes power to make, in relation to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

- (a) persons participating in any scheme within Article 19A(1), and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]
- (b) payments received by them by virtue of paragraph (1), *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

provision corresponding to any provision which (by virtue of Article 4(1) or (2) of that Order) may be made in relation to persons using such facilities, and to such payments received by them, as are mentioned in Article 4(1) of that Order. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-615a412977cb812038114738267fc2ef]

#### 20. Recovery of overpayments

In the Administration Act, insert after section 69—

> **Jobseeker's allowance**
>
> **69A. Recovery of jobseeker's allowance: severe hardship cases.**
>
> (1) Where—
>
> - (a) a severe hardship direction is revoked; and
> - (b) it is determined by an adjudication officer that—
>   - (i) whether fraudulently or otherwise, any person has misrepresented, or failed to disclose, any material fact; and
>   - (ii) in consequence of the failure of misrepresentation, payment of a jobseeker's allowance has been made during the relevant period to the person to whom the direction related,
>
>   an adjudication officer may determine that the Department is entitled to recover the amount of the payment.
>
> (2) In this section—
>
> - “severe hardship direction” means a direction given under Article 18 of the Jobseekers (Northern Ireland) Order 1995; and
> - “the relevant period” means—
>   - (a) if the revocation is under Article 18(3)(a) of that Order, the period beginning with the date of the change of circumstances and ending with the date of the revocation; and
>   - (b) if the revocation is under Article 18(3)(b) or (c) of that Order, the period during which the direction was in force.
>
> (3) Where a severe hardship direction is revoked, the Department may certify whether there has been misrepresentation of a material fact or failure to disclose a material fact.
>
> (4) If the Department certifies that there has been such misrepresentation or failure to disclose, it may certify—
>
> - (a) who made the misrepresentation or failed to make the disclosure; and
> - (b) whether or not a payment of jobseeker's allowance has been made in consequence of the misrepresentation or failure.
>
> (5) If the Department certifies that a payment has been made, it may certify the period during which a jobseeker's allowance would not have been paid but for the misrepresentation or failure to disclose.
>
> (6) A certificate under this section shall be conclusive as to any matter certified.
>
> (7) Subsections (3) and (6) to (10) of section 69 above apply to a jobseeker's allowance recoverable under subsection (1) above as they apply to a jobseeker's allowance recoverable under section 69(1) above.
>
> (8) The other provisions of section 69 above do not apply to a jobseeker's allowance recoverable under subsection (1) above.

.

### Denial of jobseeker's allowance

#### 21. Higher-level sanctions[^c-13190080f53647adfabe3eb3ae23c277]

(1)[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948] The amount of an award of a jobseeker's allowance is to be reduced in accordance with this Article in the event of a failure by the claimant which is sanctionable under this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(2) It is a failure sanctionable under this Article if a claimant— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) through misconduct loses employment as an employed earner; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) without a good reason voluntarily leaves such employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (c) without a good reason refuses or fails to apply for, or accept if offered, a situation in any employment which an employment officer has informed him is vacant or about to become vacant; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (d) without a good reason neglects to avail himself of a reasonable opportunity of employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (e) without a good reason fails to participate in any scheme within Article 19A(1) which is prescribed for the purposes of this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(3) For the purposes of paragraph (2)(b), in such circumstances as may be prescribed, including in particular where a person has been dismissed by his employer by reason of redundancy within the meaning of Article 174(1) of the Employment Rights (Northern Ireland) Order 1996 after volunteering or agreeing to be so dismissed, a person who might otherwise be treated as having left his employment voluntarily is to be treated as not having left voluntarily. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(4) Regulations are to provide for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the amount of a reduction under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the period for which such a reduction has effect, not exceeding 18 months in relation to any failure sanctionable under this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(5) Regulations under paragraph (4)(b) may in particular provide for the period of a reduction to depend on either or both of the following— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the number of failures by the claimant sanctionable under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the period between such failures. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(6) Regulations may provide— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) for cases in which no reduction is to be made under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) for a reduction under this Article made in relation to an award that is terminated to be applied to any new award made within a prescribed period of the termination. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(7) During any period for which the amount of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance is reduced under this Article by virtue of a failure by one of the claimants which is sanctionable under this Article, the allowance is payable to the other member of the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

#### 21A. Other sanctions

(1) The amount of an award of a jobseeker's allowance is to be reduced in accordance with this Article in the event of a failure by the claimant which is sanctionable under this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(2) It is a failure sanctionable under this Article if a claimant— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) without a good reason fails to comply with regulations under Article 10(1) or (1A); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) without a good reason fails to comply with regulations under Article 19A; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (c) without a good reason refuses or fails to carry out a jobseeker's direction which was reasonable having regard to his circumstances; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (d) without a good reason neglects to avail himself of a reasonable opportunity of a place on a training scheme or employment programme; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (e) without a good reason refuses or fails to apply for, or accept if offered, a place on such a scheme or programme which an employment officer has informed him is vacant or about to become vacant; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (f) without a good reason gives up a place on such a scheme or programme or fails to attend such a scheme or programme having been given a place on it; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (g) through misconduct loses a place on such a scheme or programme. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(3) But a failure is not sanctionable under this Article if it is also sanctionable under Article 21. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(4) Regulations are to provide for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the amount of a reduction under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the period for which such a reduction has effect. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(5) Regulations under paragraph (4)(b) may provide that a reduction under this Article in relation to any failure is to have effect for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) a period continuing until the claimant meets a compliance condition specified by the Department, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) a fixed period not exceeding 26 weeks which is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
  - (i) specified in the regulations, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948] determined in any case by the Department, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (c) a combination of both. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(6) In paragraph (5)(a) “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]**compliance condition *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]**” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) a condition that the failure ceases, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) a condition relating to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
  - (i) future compliance with a jobseeker's direction or any requirement imposed under Article 10(1) or (1A) or 19A, or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948] future avoidance of the failures referred to in paragraph (2)(d) to (g). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(7) A compliance condition specified under paragraph (5)(a) may be— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) revoked or varied by the Department; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) notified to the claimant in such manner as the Department may determine. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(8) The period fixed under paragraph (5)(b) may in particular depend on either or both of the following— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the number of failures by the claimant sanctionable under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the period between such failures. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(9) Regulations may provide— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) for cases in which no reduction is to be made under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) for a reduction under this Article made in relation to an award that is terminated to be applied to any new award made within a prescribed period of the termination. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(10) During any period for which the amount of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance is reduced under this Article by virtue of a failure by one of the claimants which is sanctionable under this Article, the allowance is payable to the other member of the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(11) In this Article— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) “**jobseeker's direction *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]**” means a direction given by an employment officer (in such manner as he thinks fit) with a view to achieving one or both of the following— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
  - (i) assisting the claimant to find employment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948] improving the claimant's prospects of being employed; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) “training scheme” and “employment programme” have such meaning as may be prescribed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

#### 21B. Claimants ceasing to be available for employment etc.

(1) Regulations may make provision for reduction of the amount of an award of a jobseeker's allowance other than a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance if the claimant— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) was previously entitled to such an allowance or was a member of a couple entitled to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) ceased to be so entitled by failing to comply with the condition in Article 3(2)(a) or (c) (availability for employment and actively seeking employment). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(2) Regulations may make provision for reduction of the amount of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance if one of the claimants— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) was previously entitled to a jobseeker's allowance other than a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) ceased to be so entitled by failing to comply with the condition in Article 3(2)(a) or (c). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(3) Regulations may make provision for reduction of the amount of an award of joint-claim jobseeker's allowance if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the couple were previously entitled to a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance but ceased to be so entitled by either or both of them failing to comply with the condition in Article 3(2)(a) or (c), or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) either member of the couple was a member of another couple previously entitled to such an allowance and that couple ceased to be so entitled by that person failing to comply with the condition in Article 3(2)(a) or (c). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(4) Regulations are to provide for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the amount of a reduction under this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the period for which such a reduction has effect. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(5) The period referred to in paragraph (4)(b) must not include any period after the end of the period of 13 weeks beginning with the day on which the claimant's previous entitlement ceased. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(6) Regulations under paragraph (4)(b) may in particular provide for the period of a reduction to depend on either or both of the following— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the number of occasions on which a claimant's entitlement has ceased as specified in paragraph (1), (2) or (3); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the period between such occasions. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(7) Regulations may provide for a reduction under this Article made in relation to an award that is terminated to be applied to any new award made within a prescribed period of the termination. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(8) During any period for which the amount of a joint-claim jobseeker's allowance is reduced under this Article by virtue of a failure by one of the claimants to comply with the condition in Article 3(2)(a) or (c), the allowance is payable to the other member of the couple. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

#### 21C. Hardship payments

(1) Regulations may make provision for the making of payments (“hardship payments”) by way of a jobseeker's allowance to a claimant where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) the amount of the claimant's award is reduced under Articles 21 to 21B, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) the claimant is or will be in hardship. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

(2) Regulations under this Article may in particular make provision as to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

- (a) circumstances in which a claimant is to be treated as being or not being in hardship; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (b) matters to be taken into account in determining whether a claimant is or will be in hardship; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (c) requirements or conditions to be met by a claimant in order to receive hardship payments; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (d) the amount or rate of hardship payments; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (e) the period for which hardship payments may be made; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]
- (f) whether hardship payments are recoverable. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72f67a90e06f3921279a644d58ea6948]

#### 22. Exemptions from Article 21 and 21A[^c-10f7cfbbba83165d383bfe71c152363f]

(1) Nothing in Article 21, or in regulations under that Article, shall be taken to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] authorise reduction[^c-014bc511cb2a7188229e05d3ab2623a6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] of a jobseeker's allowance merely because the claimant refuses to seek or accept employment in a situation which is vacant in consequence of a stoppage of work due to a trade dispute. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]

(2) Article *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] 21A[^M_F_ca7551e0-793e-49b8-e885-ca74c2934099] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] does not apply, in the circumstances mentioned in paragraph *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] (2)(c) to (g)[^c-682e39f5406c6f099fb7e952746bdfb8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] of that Article, if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]

- (a) a direction is in force under Article 18 with respect to the claimant; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]
- (b) he has acted in such a way as to risk— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]
  - (i) having that direction revoked under paragraph (3)(b) of Article 18; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] having the amount of his jobseeker's allowance reduced by virtue of Article 19, because *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695][^c21802251] he has failed to complete a course of training. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]

(3) Regulations shall make provision for the purpose of enabling any person of a prescribed description to accept any employed earner's employment without falling within Article *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] 21(2)(b) or (d)[^c-ae03580d432e1c3213539e8fa1487470] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] should he leave that employment voluntarily and without *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] good reason *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695] at any time during a trial period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bab4899df43a7bc5648ea2778fa65695]

(4) In such circumstances as may be prescribed, an income-based jobseeker's allowance shall be payable in respect of[^c16984941] a claimant even though Article 21 prevents payment of a jobseeker's allowance to him.

(5) A jobseeker's allowance shall be payable by virtue of paragraph (4) only if the claimant has complied with such requirements as to the provision of information as may be prescribed for the purposes of this paragraph.

(6) Regulations under paragraph (4) may, in particular, provide for a jobseeker's allowance payable by virtue of that paragraph to be—

- (a) payable at a prescribed rate;
- (b) payable for a prescribed period (which may differ from the period fixed under Article 21(2) or (3)).

(7) In paragraph (3), “trial period” has such meaning as may be prescribed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-153a71d2fb5ef5faa7de95e9314a320e]

(8) Regulations may make provision for determining, for the purposes of this Article, the day on which a person's employment is to be regarded as commencing. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-153a71d2fb5ef5faa7de95e9314a320e]
